# Luna core expansion: second bounded pass (400 new atoms)

Snapshot: **2026-08-15 UTC**. Additive research only; no application code or catalogue data was edited.

## Scope, evidence, and deduplication

I excluded all IDs in 10-luna-core-scale.md (luna-001 through luna-250), the existing catalogue, and research notes 04–06. These 400 candidates cover tokenizer-aware transforms, context lifecycle, retrieval indexing/chunking, tool schemas, multimodal compression, agent orchestration, evaluation/stopping, failure/retry controls, language-specific code-generation savings, and cross-provider workload normalization.

Dedupe procedure: normalize title, aliases, action, and fingerprint; remove punctuation/provider names; compare normalized 3-grams with all prior IDs/titles/headings; then manually review the nearest family. IDs luna2-001…luna2-400 are proposed stable canonical IDs. All are conservatively marked derived because primary sources support mechanisms while savings require the listed experiment. Any later semantic collision should be dropped, not renamed.

## Count

400 new candidates (10 families × 40).
